ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u57	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present the Government project in print 1865 concerning the proposal for new legislation on excise duties on tobacco products and the amendment of other laws. The tobacco industry is crucial for the state budget due to the large share of indirect taxes on tobacco products in the overall budgetary impact. In 2016, for example, the State's excise budget received revenue of PLN 65,7 billion, including excise duties on tobacco products - PLN 18,5 billion. Revenue from excise duties on tobacco products was 4% in 2016 more than in the previous year, but the problem of the shadow economy remains in the tobacco sector. The market for legally processed tobacco products is estimated at over PLN 27 billion, so it is a large market. In 2016, officers under the Ministry of Finance discovered a record number of 72 illegal tobacco factories, seized 44.5 million cigarettes and more than 200 tonnes of tobacco from illegal sources. The above shows how much of the problem is the production of tobacco products by illegal factories and the non-payment of taxes due to the state budget. The draft law amending the excise duty Act and the Act on the organisation of certain agricultural markets aims at taxing excise duties on the sale of tobacco dried tobacco by a producer of dried tobacco made in connection with intra-Community supply or export of that product by that entity, imposing an obligation on intermediary tobacco operators to transfer to the National Centre for Agricultural Support, KOWR, data on concentrated tobacco drought and the list of producers of such dried tobacco, imposing an obligation to keep a register of tobacco dried to taxable persons who store non-taxed tobacco droughts outside the tax warehouse, introducing a new form of excise security, i.e. mortgage on real estate, allow the lodging of excise security by third parties instead of an intermediary tobacco operator and an entity representing a foreign trader without a branch established in the territory of the country or including such an entity, to reduce the upper limit of the flat-rate security lodged by intermediary tobacco operators or entities representing a foreign trader from the current PLN 30 million to PLN 15 million and to establish a register of producers of raw tobacco kept by KOWR. In addition, the project aims to introduce rules allowing checks to be carried out on the monitoring of tobacco leaf cultivation and the destruction of the cultivation at the cost of tobacco cultivation without obtaining an entry in the register of producers of raw tobacco and on a surface exceeding the notified area, the obligation on producers of raw tobacco to provide once a year information on the area of cultivation, production and disposal of raw tobacco and the contracts concluded for the disposal of raw tobacco, as well as on penalties for non-compliance with the rules on timely transmission of information on cultivation, production and disposal of raw tobacco. The purpose of the changes, High Izbo, is to seal the circulation of dried tobacco leaves. Recommended solutions will allow monitoring of the circulation of tobacco drought from the time of cultivation of tobacco leaf to the actual consumption of tobacco products. It is expected that the proposed measures will reduce the weight of raw tobacco delivered to tobacco manufacturers operating against the provisions of the Excise Tax Act and improve the operating conditions of legal intermediaries. In view of the above, it is justified to introduce the proposed amendment to the excise duty Act. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u59	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! Dear Minister, On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I have the honour to present a position on a government draft law amending the excise duty law and the law on the organisation of certain agricultural markets. As a result of the amendment of the excise duty Act introduced as from 1 January 2016, the excise security lodged by intermediary tobacco operators carrying out an economic activity in the field of tobacco drought is intended to ensure that tax obligations are covered. As a result of these and other activities, the shadow economy in the cigarette market is already falling. The tobacco industry is crucial for the state budget due to the high share of excise duty on tobacco products in the overall budgetary impact. In 2015, the State's excise budget received revenues for tobacco products of PLN 17.8 billion, while in 2016 those revenues amounted to PLN 18.5 billion. So this is an increase of 0.7 billion PLN. Reinforcing control services should also include more effective control of tobacco drought. Illegal trade in tobacco products is one of the most profitable spheres of the grey zone. This draft law intends to amend two laws: the excise duty law and the law on the organisation of certain agricultural markets. The amendments to the Excise Tax Act are mainly intended to seal the tax system for the marketing of tobacco drought and to facilitate the pursuit of business activities for legal entities legally trading tobacco drought. In line with the new proposal in the article. 9 sales of tobacco dried with intra-Community supply or export without excise duty will only be made by the tax warehouse operator, by a group of producers of tobacco dried by farmers or by an intermediary tobacco operator and not, as yet, by a farmer. This will preserve competitiveness in the tobacco export market. The sale of tobacco dried by the farmer alone will be possible, but it will entail an obligation to pay excise duties. The proposed amendments will also allow for information on the weight of raw tobacco delivered to the purchaser under contract, as well as on the weight of raw tobacco collected broken down by group of tobacco varieties. Having information about the same object from different sources allows us to carry out so-called. cross-checks during which the quantity sold with the quantity of concentrated tobacco dried will be verified. The solution proposed in the Article. 63 provides that, instead of the intermediary tobacco operator and the representative of the foreign trader or together with the latter, the required excise security may be lodged by a third party in order to facilitate the pursuit of the business activities of the tobacco traders. In line with the demands of the intermediaries, the upper limit of the flat-rate security lodged by these operators is expected to be reduced from PLN 30 million to PLN 15 million. The Act also introduces a new form of excise security, which is a mortgage on real estate. The above amendment meets the demands of intermediary tobacco products in this respect. The amendments to the Act on the organisation of certain agricultural markets aim to seal the marketing of dried tobacco leaves, hereinafter referred to as tobacco raw materials, in Poland. Control of tobacco cultivation and the production and sale of raw tobacco, as well as supervision of tobacco cultivation and production and disposal of raw tobacco are introduced. The monitoring and control mentioned above aims to limit the supply of raw tobacco to illegally operating tobacco factories. The draft law also sets out the procedure for the destruction of raw tobacco in the event of a decision by the producer of the raw tobacco. Regulations have also been introduced which will apply to the cessation of tobacco cultivation by its manufacturer. The imposition of sanctions is proportionate to the losses suffered by the Polish economy as a result of the action of the shadow economy in the trade in tobacco raw materials. Rules on penalties for non-compliance with the rules set out in the draft aim to ensure the reliability of the data transmitted [[♪ The bell ♪]] for monitoring tobacco cultivation as a result of the need to reduce the supply of raw tobacco to tobacco producing plants. The proposed solutions impose a number of obligations, but are very beneficial to the tobacco industry itself, which is why the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice supports the Government's draft law on amending the excise tax law and the Act on the organisation of certain agricultural markets contained in print No. 1865. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u73	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present the Government's draft law on the amendment of the act on the tax on goods and services concerning the so-called. split payment mechanism. The split payment mechanism is an innovative way to reduce the pathology of VAT fraud, specifically the so-called. a disappearing taxpayer who has developed in the country over recent years, but also in other Member States of the European Union due to the characteristics of cross-border VAT settlements within the European Union. The split payment mechanism was analysed in the European Union documents of 2010. Based on these concepts, the government has prepared the solution proposed in this Act. This solution is based on a change in the technology used in the in-bank clearing system. The change of this technology will enable the taxable person to pay the VAT invoice to the counterparty in two financial streams - in the net stream and in the VAT stream. The net stream will be available to the counterparty in a traditional way, without restrictions, while the VAT payment stream will be embedded in a VAT account with limited flexibility in the disposal of these funds. These funds will in principle be available to the supplier's account, including VAT, or to the tax office for payment of value added tax. The mechanism proposed by this law makes it impossible to select money from this VAT account easily, is practically eliminated. This is a problem of the current system - amounts received from the contractor by criminals are simply transferred abroad and there is virtually no possibility of reaching these amounts. This split payment system makes it difficult or eliminates easy robbing of the budget from the VAT due. The proposed mechanism is a voluntary mechanism. We do not force entrepreneurs to apply this mechanism, but through the incentive system introduced by the VAT Act we will encourage entrepreneurs to use it at least in cases where the trader may have doubts as to the integrity of its contractor, its supplier. These incentives, which we have proposed in the Act, is the accelerated refund of VAT from the tax office to this VAT account, the absence of VAT sanctions, which is an important risk factor in the conduct of business today, the exemption from joint and several liability, which is also a difficult area for taxpayers, the possibility of applying a pre-payment account - if the taxpayer knows that he has an excess payment in this account, he can pay the tax and count on the account, i.e. the state budget will pay off part of the cost of money during that taxpayer's time - and in certain circumstances there will be no higher amount of interest for late payment in respect of VAT arrears. This is a mix of incentives that we consider appropriate at this stage. We are considering, this would be further steps, next steps to include some of the most sensitive sectors in the mandatory system, but these are further steps, also depending on the agreement at the level of the European Commission, which we will present in the near future. The expected effects of this mechanism are to seal the VAT system, increase the certainty of economic turnover, improve the competitive position of honest entrepreneurs vis-à-vis those entrepreneurs who finance their pricing policy simply by not paying VAT, eliminate joint and several liability for the fraud of counterparties, and this is a problem for both traders and tax authorities, which have to apply such an assessment instrument to taxpayers. We will also reduce the scale and necessity of thorough verification of the counterparties that the taxpayer considers to be suspicious. The financial effects of this law are estimated very modestly next year, because the benefits and disadvantages bear, but in the following year, 2019, the effect of sealing this system is estimated at already PLN 2 billion. We therefore submit this bill to the High House for consideration. If there are questions, of course we are ready to answer. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u74	Thank you very much. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. Please take the floor of Mr Sylvester Tulayev, the Law and Justice Club.

I would like to remind you that in 2017 a system for monitoring the carriage of goods by road was introduced, and further work is being done on the extension of the system operating a single control file. Today we have another bill, very important, very necessary. It's a bill about a split payment mechanism. The essence of this solution is that the payment for the purchased goods or services is made in such a way that the payment corresponding to the net sales value is paid by the purchaser to the supplier's clearing account or is otherwise settled, while the remaining payment corresponding to the amount of the value of the goods and services tax is paid to the supplier's special account, the VAT account. The split payment mechanism is considered to be one of the solutions aimed at combating fraud and tax fraud, eliminating the risk of taxpayers disappearing together with those paid to them by counterparties not subject to VAT. This mechanism is intended to make it difficult or even impossible for fraud to arise already at the stage of the transaction itself. The solution proposed in the project assumes voluntaryity in the application of the split payment mechanism. The initiative in this respect is left to the buyer of goods or services, but his use of this clearing method will have certain consequences for both the buyer and the supplier of the goods or services. In order to enable all taxable persons purchasing goods or services to make payments under the split payment mechanism, the VAT account will be established by banks or cooperative savings and credit banks for each entity holding a clearing account and an equivalent name account for the purpose of business activity in the SKOK. Importantly, this project is not interfering with constitutional property rights. Both the VAT account itself and the funds collected on that account will still belong to the taxable person of the tax on goods and services. The taxable person will be able to dispose of these funds at his discretion, although for limited purposes: payment of the VAT liability to the tax office or payment of an amount corresponding to the VAT amount of the invoice received from his counterparty. Mrs. Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, This bill is very important. It favours this, as I said, to seal the tax system in Poland, so that the amounts due from VAT which are due to the Polish State are primarily for all residents, all citizens. Therefore, as Law and Justice, as Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, I request that this project be further examined in committees and that this much-needed bill be finally adopted. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u77	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, the Civic Platform has the honour to present an opinion on the draft law amending the Act on VAT on goods and services and certain other laws, print No 1864. The aim of the project is to prevent tax evasion on goods and services, thereby ensuring greater stability of the proceeds of this tax. However, it should be borne in mind that sealing solutions should not hinder business activity. So taking care of the interests of the State Treasury, the interests of the Treasury should not lead us to lose sight of the interests of entrepreneurs. The draft law provides for the introduction of the so-called mechanism. divided payment. The taxable person purchasing the goods or services will be able to decide whether he will benefit from this form of liability regulation. Taxpayers will be able to use this mechanism in an alternative way, i.e. some invoices will be able to regulate on a split payment basis, some in a regular or traditional way. This mechanism consists in the payment of an amount corresponding in whole or in part to the amount of tax resulting from the invoice received being made to the VAT account and the payment of all or part of the amount corresponding to the net sales value resulting from the invoice received is made to the bank account or to the account in the cooperative credit and savings account for which the VAT account is kept. The split payment mechanism can indeed be an effective instrument to combat tax evasion in the area of goods and services tax, as this tax paid to the supplier or service provider will be credited to an isolated account and, as a rule, to the tax authority. Some of the incentives you've been talking about here are the Minister's. However, it should be stressed that the solution contained in the project also poses significant risks. It can seriously affect the financial liquidity of businesses, as the supplier or service provider will only be able to have at its disposal the net amount of the receivable received. The amount of the tax will be frozen on a separate VAT account, and it is clear that the taxable person can or can freely operate from the moment of receipt of the funds until the tax is paid. Such a situation will be ruled out here, because it is clear that taxpayers need the full amount of dues, including the amount of taxes on goods and services, in order to fulfil their obligations. Providers and service providers may be subject to various pressures, e.g. pressure on the fact that if a respected counterparty does not agree to a reduction in the price, I will give it a split payment, and that is no longer in line with the principles of equal competition. In practice, agreements may be concluded whereby payment for goods or services will be made in a traditional form, precisely as a result of such pressure as I have said. In addition, it should be stressed that the introduction of a split payment mechanism will be costly primarily for entrepreneurs. It will be expensive for banks, but banks will switch it to entrepreneurs. Given the above-mentioned characteristics of the split payment - and that is what the Minister did not say - this mechanism is not widely used in the European Union. He's practically in Italy, [[♪ The bell ♪]] in the Czech Republic, and to a limited extent. Now Romania is making this alliance. It would therefore be important to consider how, in the course of legislative work - because such measures are possible - to eliminate the disadvantages of split payments so that all the negative consequences that I have said here do not appear, especially since the Ministry has some prospects for this payment. Thank you. [[Applause]]

The joint and several liability of both parties to the transaction is envisaged. The application of the split payment mechanism is that, first, the payment of an amount corresponding to all or part of the amount of tax resulting from the invoice received is made to the VAT account. The payment of all or part of the amount corresponding to the net sales value of the invoice received shall either be made to a bank account or to an account in the cooperative credit and savings account for which the VAT account is kept or shall be settled by other means. For each billing account, the bank will have to keep a separate VAT account to pay the amount corresponding to all or part of the amount of the tax on goods and services, to pay the amounts of the tax on goods and services by taxable persons, to transfer funds from another VAT account to the holder of the VAT account held in the same bank and to refund the tax. Of course, there are other technical issues. If there is insufficient funds in the billing account to pay the amount corresponding to the gross value of the sale indicated in the transfer notice, the bank shall not carry out the transfer. Upon receipt of the cash for an amount corresponding to the amount of the tax on goods and services indicated in the transfer notice, the bank that maintains the taxable person's settlement account shall charge that account with an amount equal to the amount of the tax on goods and services and shall recognise that amount of the VAT account of the taxable person. At this point, we have our attention. In general, the implementation of the split payment mechanism will reduce the liquidity of clearing entities in this way their claims. In short, they will not be able to have their own funds collected on a separate VAT account. The question arises as to whether it is true that payments such as, for example, cannot be regulated from the account. personal salaries, social and health contributions, income tax advances, etc. In general, there will be a restriction on the freedom to dispose of the taxpayer's financial resources. In addition, possible payments must also be partially authorised by the tax office. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u105	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Thank you for asking. I will try to discuss a few threads in your questions together. With regard to the start of this, i.e. the decision to use the split payment mechanism by the couple supplier - the buyer, this decision is actually taken together. [[And it's in the regulations somewhere?]] If the supplier excludes the possibility to pay under the split payment mechanism, then there are appropriate civil law instruments which may result in such a mechanism not being applied, in the entire civil law instrument, which allows such provisions to be included. whether under the general terms and conditions of entry into contracts, or in individual contracts that can simply eliminate this type of payment from the supplier's offer. However, it is clear that the elimination of such a priori transfer in the split payment mechanism may raise doubts for the buyer as to the tax fairness of the supplier, and the whole mechanism is embedded in this element of doubt. We cannot criticize this voluntary one, saying that it is a flaw in this project, because it makes sense for this dishonest taxpayer to be idealized from the trading system. What you're describing as a flaw... This system, unfortunately, is an element of this system an advantage. The customer should be able to approach the supplier honestly and tell him: I want to pay you in a split payment, and if this type of delivery and payment are not of interest to you, then I do not trade with you because I fear joint responsibility, responsibility resulting from case law and the involvement in the merry-go-round. So what you're describing as a flaw is the heart of this project. The aim is to protect an honest taxpayer who wants to demonstrate care and security, so that he simply does not deal with a potentially dishonest entity. So, as far as the very fact of making a decision is concerned, who is taking it, this decision is common, with emphasis on the fact that we are giving the buyer an instrument of protection from an unfair supplier. This is the heart of this project. As to whether it is an automatic or manual mechanism, this question cannot be answered: yes or no, because it is more complicated. If, in the financial and accounting system, the taxpayer chooses to use the split payment mechanism, the mechanism will be viewed as virtually automatic from the point of view of transfer services, while the decision to use it itself will be sewn up in the decision of the accounting officer, the financial director or the President whether or not to apply the mechanism. At the level of the transfer and at the technical level of the Elixir message it is a manual system. The automatic system is often understood, also in European Union documents, as a system in which the bank takes responsibility for dividing the financial stream into these two variables. This system where the banks have, so to speak, power over the sharing of payments, we do not introduce, and in this sense He's not automatic. As far as the thread of payment congestion is concerned, it is a very complicated subject. We're in the process of analyzing this phenomenon. We also have contacts with organisations that analyse this issue in depth. We hope to conduct in-depth surveys on the phenomenon of payment congestion. It is obvious, for example, that players with a strong economic position abuse their position by extending payment deadlines. It is obvious that there is a completely negative situation when even the interest paid by these stronger players for extending payment deadlines, then, on the other hand, are invoiced to the weaker counterparty to compensate for this. These things are known to us and we are in the process of developing such a set of rules, both on income tax and VAT, which are to address this issue comprehensively. However, I would like to point out that the simple methods used during the Civil Platform's term of office to throw out unpaid invoices led to the collapse of part of the construction industry. We do not plan to introduce such primitive provisions, because they are destructive in the case of this weaker part of the economy. What happened back then was a scandal. Unfortunately, in order to address the problem of payment congestion correctly, we would need to create a certain indicator, a balance of payments index per company, and here we come to the problem of complications of tax law. This means that in order to address the problem of payment congestion well, it would be necessary to build a phenomenally complicated system of assessment of whether the company is holding payments, whether it is not holding them, and only there is for example. the victim of holding at an earlier stage of the supply chain. We are between the complexity of the rules and the solution to the problem, but I would like to say that the split payment mechanism introduces the principle of payment in the invoice mechanism. This means that the company accounting system, also at the interface of a strong entity - a weak entity, will be more transparent because we will be able to easily identify the pace of handling the invoices in question. Today, settlements between companies can be carried out in a cumulative manner, i.e. one transfer into multiple invoices, including settlement of discounts, compensations, etc. This makes the system not transparent. The split payment mechanism forces you to make a transfer per invoice, because the invoice number will be a variable of the elixir message, which makes the whole payment system more transparent. If we are talking about congestion, I would also like to say that the mechanism in which the company benefits from this VAT revenue in the period up to the time of payment of value added tax applies to those most often highly favoured in the market. Who's got the excess VAT? As a rule, the excess VAT on the account has entities that collect payments more quickly from their customers and pay their suppliers later. These entities are most often strong, dominant entities, which are able to force such relations with counterparties, and we do not see the need for these entities to think very long about their problem caused by the split payment mechanism. However, there are entities on the market that have a financial situation or a business model that is not optimal and financing non-vatovous commitments from the economic value of VAT for the sale of invoices. As far as these companies are concerned, first of all, there is a limit to how far we can go with preferences in legislation itself. I think we've reached our maximum, we can't imagine addressing the problem of this type of company, which, for example. They finance their VAT salaries to get there by the end of the month, and then they find VAT money to pay. We are not in a position to address this problem legally. [[But that's the truth.]] However, we are working with state financial institutions to build an adequate offer of bridge financing that will compensate for the liquidity gap in certain situations where it appears. I believe that companies that have this kind of liquidity problem should occur or take advantage of some kind of restructuring advice, because this type of business model is dangerous for both themselves and their employees and the trust fund, because the slightest note of liquidity makes them not pay either ZUS or VAT or salary. I perfectly understand life and also perfectly understand that uncertainty about the integrity of the contractor also has costs for the economy. It's very expensive. There are industries that pay X% their turnover only in order to spend on financial institutions such as banks and insurance, insurance companies for guarantees to enable them to trade, and these guarantees year by year were increasingly expensive for these sectors. Why? Because there were mass frauds in a given sector and no insurer wanted to guarantee this turnover for normal prices because it was never known whether the other side was fair or unfair. In this project, we are not able to quantify it, but we know empirically that there is a huge cost for the economy to guarantee uncertain trading. The split payment mechanism causes these costs to be eliminated from the economy. We didn't quantify that, but we know that's the result. We have industries that even demand us to introduce this mechanism because it will free up the market and contractors will only be trading with each other. Therefore, we did not quantify and enter these advantages because they are macroeconomically very difficult to establish. However, at this stage, as it falls from the hall here, this is life, this is life, that these costs are borne by the economy and are unnecessary, they do not cause any real GDP growth. This is the lost cost of uncertainty in the system and this system, reducing uncertainty, will have a positive effect on the whole economy. It is burdened with a very long list of conditions which in principle are not met by taxpayers, while the split payment mechanism and reimbursement to this particular account will make this possibility more efficient. So, as far as you can talk about the fact that legislation doesn't shorten the deadline for payment, in reality, in the business reality, availability of the deadline for 25 days for reimbursement will increase. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u107	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation, I have the honour to present a report to the committee on a draft government bill amending the Maritime Labour Act and some other laws. The Marshal of the Sejm sent it on 15 September this year. Draft law in print No 1833 to the Committee on Maritime Affairs and Inland Navigation for first reading, with a recommendation to submit a report by 9 October this year. Therefore, the committee at its meeting on 27 September this year. carried out the first reading and examined this draft law and adopted the report. The project aims to implement European Union law. It concerns the implementation in Polish law of Directive 2015/1794 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 6 October 2015 and the provisions of the 2006 Maritime Labour Convention, called MLC, indicating the need for regular inspections of the working and living conditions of seafarers on ships flying the national flag. Implementation of amendments to Regulation 2.5 of the MLC: The purpose of repatriation is to clearly determine the extent of the costs of repatriation and to indicate that, where a ship owner of Polish nationality or the provider of safeguards in the event of abandonment of a seafarer does not take action to repatriate a seafarer, the repatriate shall be organised by the Director of the Maritime Authority responsible for the port of the ship in cooperation with the Consul of the Republic of Poland. Further proposals implement amendments to: the obligation for the ship owner to hold insurance or other financial security in the event of the abandonment of a seafarer, the obligation for the ship to hold a certificate confirming the abovementioned. the financial security issued by the provider of the security, specifying when the seafarer is deemed to have been abandoned. The draft law also lays down rules for the inspection of ships other than vessels referred to in Article. 92. 1 and 3 amended law, i.e. vessels with a gross tonnage of less than 500 whose shipowners have not applied for a certificate together with a declaration or a provisional certificate. The obligation to carry out inspections on such ships is now laid down in the Maritime Safety Act. Such inspections shall be carried out on the occasion of ship safety inspections, as practice shows, but such a solution does not ensure an adequate level of enforcement of the MLC provisions, and it is therefore necessary to introduce a dedicated inspection under the principles set out in MLC Regulation 5.1.4. The proposed arrangements include a delegation to the Minister responsible for maritime affairs to determine, by means of a regulation, the procedures for carrying out ship inspections. The fines imposed in the case of sailing by vessels which do not have adequate documents confirming compliance with the requirements for working conditions and life at sea are laid down - the MLC certificate or the provisional certificate - and the failure to submit the ship to an appropriate inspection. The fines resulting from the non-compliance with the provisions on the obligation of the company to have insurance or other financial liability in the event of abandonment have also been supplemented. Implementation of the EU Directive in Polish law is aimed at, among others, the protection of employees in the event of the insolvency of their employer, the establishment of a European Works Council, the establishment of a procedure for informing and consulting employees in undertakings or groups of undertakings of Community scope, and the establishment of a general framework for informing and consulting employees. During the work on the project, amendments were tabled clarifying the provisions of the draft law and of a legislative and editorial nature. The Commission accepted all the amendments tabled. The Commission adopted without objection a report on the draft law. I ask the High House to accept the report of the committee in print 1845. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u109	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Then he deserves protection, care from the Polish state, even in the farthest corner of the globe. This is not only a matter of his civil relations with the ship owner, the ship owner is not exempted from any duties here, but in a situation where these obligations are not fulfilled, it is also the Polish state - it is specifically indicated that the Director of the Maritime Office is coordinating with the consul - he has his duties to his citizens, to the seafarers, to take care of them, to ensure that they return home and then to pursue claims against the party who was responsible for this, i.e. the ship owner who left such a sailor. Finally, the Act guarantees, ensures that these guarantees on the part of shipowners are real and not fictional, that they have financial security, whether in the form of insurance or other financial security, to cover any claims for the costs of bringing a sailor home or for failing to pay a salary or any claims that are attributable to a sailor to cover the costs related to his illness, damage to the body, do not give God, or such basic costs of living somewhere far abroad, where he did not find himself because he went on a trip, only because the same ship owner hired him to work, and consequently there are bilateral obligations: not only an employee vis-à-vis an employer, a special employer, in such a situation, greater than an employer vis-à-vis an employee in a situation where this work takes place within a few kilometers of his home, in Poland, where everyone can leave work and return to their home. A sailor left abroad has no such possibility, is exposed to organisational difficulties, to costs. It is very good that the State will help him in these difficulties if he fails to meet his obligations, to claim the costs he has to pay later and at the same time to ensure that the same company is prepared to bear such costs in order to meet its obligations so that the principle of freedom to conclude contracts is realistic, i.e. their execution was guaranteed and indeed the weaker party to the agreement was sure that its rights were guaranteed that it would not be left to itself. Therefore, the Law and Justice Club supports the bill submitted. Thank you. [[Applause]]

In practice, the provisions of the Convention are applicable to international maritime transport, regulating and covering essential issues relating to the mutual rights and obligations of shipowners, intermediaries and, above all, seafarers. These changes were particularly of interest to seafarers working on board ships, as the introduction of new international rules absolutely guarantees not only fair employment conditions for all seafarers but also the right to work in accordance with safety standards. Among the most important changes, it should be stressed that seafarers should be repatriated where the scope of the repatriation costs is clearly defined and that the entity required to cover these costs, i.e. The ship owner. The entity responsible for the recovery of repatriation costs shall also be identified in the event that the costs are not covered by the ship owner or have not been covered by the financial security provider in the event of abandonment of the seafarer. It seems appropriate that they will be directors of maritime offices. In addition, what should also be considered appropriate and desirable in order to ensure the safety of the work and life of seafarers is introduced, the obligation for the ship owner to have insurance in the event of the abandonment of the seafarer, as confirmed by the relevant certificate of financial security. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u113	Dear Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! They were incorporated into the Polish legal system by the Law of 23 September 2016 on the ratification of the 2014 amendments to the Maritime Labour Convention. The current legal situation has prevented seafarers from being included in the implementing acts or allowed the Member States of the European Union not to apply them to them. The new arrangements align the working conditions of seafarers with the standards for workers on land and introduce amendments to the 2014 Maritime Labour Convention, which provide adequate protection for seafarers, inter alia. in case of illness, injury or death. The new rules are designed to improve the working and living conditions of seafarers. Points 4 and 5 of Article shall provide for this to be the case. 1: The financial liability of shipowners. They introduce an obligation for the shipowners to have financial security for claims arising out of sickness, injury or death of a seafarer. The existence of a financial security will be confirmed by the certificate issued by the provider of the security carried on board, which is a requirement of the MLC as amended by the 2014 amendments. The project also sets out rules for inspections of ships not subject to an MLC certificate. A penalty of between 5 and 10 000 is foreseen for the infringement. zł. They will be measured, among others. for shipping without appropriate documents confirming compliance with the requirements for working conditions and life at sea. In addition, today's amendment to the Maritime Labour Act of 5 August 2015 will introduce in Article. 62a (2) definition of the situation in which a seafarer is deemed to have been abandoned. This will be the case if the ship owner fails to cover the costs of repatriation, leaves the seafarer without reasonable means of subsistence or otherwise ceases to carry out his duties arising from the employment relationship and, in particular, does not pay remuneration for work for at least 2 months. In conclusion, the proposal aims to implement the Directive of the European Parliament and the amendments of 2014 to the Maritime Labour Convention of 2006. The effect of the regulation is to provide additional insurance protection for seafarers. The Kukiz Club15 recognises that additional safeguards are a good step. They will improve the working conditions and safety of seafarers and their families. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u129	Thank you very much, Marshal. Dear Marshal! High Izbo! It has been my honor to present on behalf of the President a bill amending the Law on Free Legal Assistance. This is a project that responds to the expectations of reforming this system, which was set up under the Law of 5 August 2015, 2 years of de facto functioning of this system of free legal assistance showed that it needs to be improved in order to improve the effectiveness of the benefits provided under this system to beneficiaries who can and could benefit from these services, and that it is worth expanding the range of services offered also about mediation or civic guidance. The data for the previous year, in which for the first time a system of free legal aid operated throughout the year, indicates that 237 thousand. benefits which were granted in 882 points led by lawyers and lawyers, as well as over 140 thousand. the benefits provided in 642 points run by NGOs, the average duration of such advice was between 15 minutes and 1 hour. It was up to 76% all the advice given. It is also worth noting that these advices, which lasted up to half an hour, represented more than 42% in this group all advice given, while more than 9% advice provided was advice that lasted no longer than 15 minutes. This indicates that the average number of advice provided per hour at one point of free legal assistance is one advice. One advice per hour at one point It's extremely small. Therefore, in view of this demand, the President decided to present a project to activate the actions and make more effective use of the opportunities created in 2015. The first group of amendments, which concerns the Law on Free Legal Aid and Legal Education, primarily concerns the extension of the circle of entities which are entitled to receive this assistance. This directory of entities will be expanded by the amendment to include persons who receive monthly income not exceeding 2 000. PLN per family member, and in the case of persons who run the household alone, 3 000. zł. In addition, the benefit will be granted to those who have received a family benefit, to provide education for the first child under the The catalogue of authorised entities has also been extended to include persons who are repatriates and within 5 years of the entry into force of the law will obtain Polish citizenship, as well as people who return from long-term migration lasting more than 5 years. They will also be able, regardless of their income, to obtain benefits within the framework of free legal assistance within a year from their return to Poland. The catalogue of benefits that can be obtained under free legal assistance has also been extended. First of all, it was also enriched with the possibility of obtaining legal advice at the stage of judicial, judicial and administrative proceedings or preparatory proceedings, and - unlike the original submission - the possibility to draw up a draft letter, not a procedural letter, but a letter which will aim at e.g. obtain a lawyer or a lawyer from the office. In addition, the project also offers the possibility to obtain support and advice in the field of tax, foreign exchange or economic law, although it continues to support the possibility of excluding from this range of benefits which are linked to the pursuit of business activities. Only, as under the 2015 Act, can advice be obtained as part of the establishment of an economic activity. The catalogue of services, which will be available as part of free assistance, has also been extended by free mediation. This is an expression or rather a response to the need for non-process dispute resolution in order to relieve the courts on the one hand and, on the other hand, to prevent many different conflict situations before they occur. Mediations will be provided at the same points where free legal services will be provided. Therefore, it will be provided by those who are competent to prepare and experience in this field. The Act also introduces a whole new kind of counseling, which is civic counseling. It is counseling, which has received great support and has gained great popularity in other European countries as well as in Poland. This is guidance, which is not limited to legal issues only, but also concerns issues such as: related to loans, various types of financial commitments, as well as related issues e.g. with apartments. Citizens' advice will be carried out under the law by the assistance points entrusted to non-governmental organisations. In this case, it is primarily about exploiting the potential of NGOs and their great experience in this area. The proposed amendment also allows older people to use time more flexibly, which is intended for free legal assistance. Within a five-day period of four hours, every day, on duty... It will be possible to specialize in these on-calls so that people who need very specialized advice in some area can come forward, or it will be advice that is addressed to certain groups of beneficiaries, e.g. such persons who are at risk of losing their apartment in connection with the reprivatization of tenements. In view of the changes introduced, there is also no provision for a burden on the State budget, because the proposed law simply provides for a more efficient use of the funds already available for free legal aid. In the framework of this amendment, it is planned that the limits resulting from the Article. The 28th 2015 Act will not increase. In view of the above, High Izbo, I would like to ask you to welcome this very important and important initiative of the President. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u131	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The ignorance of the law is bad. This truth, derived from Latin parishes, expresses one of the basic principles of the law. Close to it is another paraemia, which means that no one can justify his actions or omissions that he does not know the law. In doctrine they are the basis of the principle that knowledge of the law has been common since Roman times. It would be difficult to live a stable social and economic life without such an assumption. However, the number of regulations is constantly increasing, from local to international level, with particular emphasis on the rules created in this Chamber. In many respects, help remains necessary, professional assistance provided by lawyers, by those who have taken on the task of continuing learning in the knowledge of the law. There are among us, in communities in our districts, however, those who, due to their material status or the situation of life, are unable to obtain professional legal assistance. The Law on Free Legal Assistance and Legal Education of 2015 was to address this problem. Its aim was to create mechanisms for access to legal advice by people in a more difficult situation of life and to guarantee access to legal information for all. Unfortunately, already at the time of its adoption, mechanisms were visible that did not guarantee the effective achievement of its objectives, and two years of its application showed further areas that were worth the reaction of the legislator. In my office, but I'm guessing a lot of you came to the offices, and people said they were at that point of help, and they didn't get that help. And that is what needs to be fixed, and the presidential project is facing these changes, these challenges. The Presidential draft amendment provides for access to free mediation at the pre-trial stage. Such a solution offers the possibility of resolving conflicts even before judicial proceedings are opened and the number of cases which need to be resolved by the court is reduced. The Act will be supplemented by civic guidance in accordance with the project under consideration. As a result, those who have obtained advice and who have difficulty understanding the complex legal language will be able to supplement their knowledge. Another amendment is the introduction of provisions enabling legal information to be obtained in points of free legal assistance in the course of proceedings and not, as has been the case so far, only at the pre-trial stage. A catalogue of cases which are exempted under the current law will also be amended and will be limited to matters strictly related to the conduct of business, but with the exception of preparation for its start-up. The amendments also extend the catalogue of persons entitled to legal aid under the Act, including: for unemployed persons, disabled persons, indebted persons or persons in respect of whom an eviction claim has been filed or who have been terminated the lease of a dwelling. In addition, legal aid will be available to persons whose income per family member does not exceed 2 000. PLN or 3000. - in the case of single householders, and those persons who have been given a parental benefit for the first child on the basis of the Law on State aid to raise children, i.e. the Family 500+. The right to obtain free legal assistance will be based on the submission of the relevant document or the submission of a statement under the strictest responsibility. The Presidential project also introduces a novelty of the obligation to redirect a person entitled by a person providing free legal assistance to other aid schemes, provided that the problem presented by him is not merely legal. The Starosts will be obliged to publish a list of individuals who conduct free family, psychological, pedagogical, consumer rights, patient rights, social security, crisis intervention, family or addiction violence. The draft law presented by the President also seeks to regulate issues relating to the area of law or to the issue of free legal assistance or civic advice provided during the on-call period, and to identify the different locations of individual on-call units consisting of a single point in order to distribute them in such a way as to guarantee privacy and confidentiality, but also to facilitate the provision of assistance to customers of other support schemes. The Act also introduces provisions preventing the applicant from refusing to appoint a defender or a representative of his own motion in legal proceedings because of the applicant's use of free legal assistance or free of charge civic advice. According to the Parliamentary Club, the law and justice introduced by the bill is logical and coherent and, of course, we judge it [[♪ The bell ♪]] Positive. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u133	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, the Civic Platform has the honour to present its position on the presidential draft of amendments to the Law on Free Legal Assistance and Legal Education. This proposal provides for changes which are essentially expressed in the extension of the scope of the subject matter and the law in question, a change in the procedure for the selection of non-governmental organisations providing unpaid legal assistance, the possibility for non-governmental organisations to provide free of charge civic guidance and the possibility for local authorities to define specific points of free assistance. In principle, the very idea of extending the catalogue of entities entitled to receive free legal assistance should be assessed positively, as it remains in the spirit of the assumptions drawn up in 2015 by the Government of the Civic Platform Act, whose regulation would lead to the creation of mechanisms for access to legal advice by all those persons who, due to their material status or their living situation, are unable to obtain professional legal assistance. However, the broadly defined catalogue proposed in the presidential project of entities entitled to obtain free legal advice should be reviewed in the context of the constitutional principle of equality criteria, in the light of the possible effects that this regulation may have on the legal services market, and finally in the context of the impact on the functioning of points of free legal assistance themselves. The proposed regulations may also make those more in need of assistance longer await advice. There is also doubt about the possibility for local authorities to define specific points of free legal aid. In fact, the specialisation of points poses a risk of limiting the availability of legal assistance to those in real need of it. Unpaid legal assistance points to enable them to carry out the tasks entrusted in a realistic and efficient way should respond as universally as possible to citizens' needs for legal assistance. In addition, indicate it is necessary that the introduction of the possibility to specialise in points of free legal aid should be combined with the answer to the question of how people who run such specialized points will be identified and on the basis of which criteria local authorities will decide on their specialization. It is also doubtful that the voivodeship is assigned competence to draw up a list of non-governmental organisations entitled to carry out points of free legal assistance and civic guidance. In the light of the proposed solution, NGOs will apply for inclusion in the list led by the voivode. The consequence of such a form of regulation, and probably the aim in itself, unfortunately, is the centralisation of the system, because this certainly does not lead to an improvement of the system of free legal assistance. The draft amendment also introduces, in addition to legal advice, civic guidance. The Civic Platform Club does not oppose the very idea of civic guidance and its implementation, but it should be noted that the proposed change will contribute to reducing the potential of points of free legal assistance on legal advice. In addition, it should be recommended to allocate a separate envelope to finance this part of the task. High Izbo! The proposed changes, which are intended to make full use of the potential of the point-of-free aid scheme, must at the same time involve the introduction of promotion instruments at central and local level and should also focus on the introduction of tools to implement the demands for legal education. The Civic Platform Parliamentary Club, recognising the need to discuss the design of a system of free legal assistance, taking into account past experience in the operation of points of free assistance, and recognising the need to modify the proposal proposed in the presidential amendment to the Law of Solutions, requests that the project be submitted to the committees for further work. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u184	Thank you very much. Ms Urszula Pasławska, Polish People's Party, has the floor.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u185	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! I have the pleasure on behalf of the club of the Polish People's Party to present my position on the establishment of the 2018 Year of Remembrance of the Wielkopolska Uprising. The club, of course, supports the draft resolution. We are involved in a valuable initiative to honor one of the most important and probably the most underestimated events in the entire national history of Poland, the only insurrection in the history of Poland, which really ended in full success. We would also like to emphasise the huge contribution of Polish peasants, farmers to the victory of the Greater Poland Uprising. Just peasants and agricultural workers were the core of the insurgent army, as many as 64% of the soldiers. Only 36% of the troops came from other backgrounds: intelligence, craftsmen, merchants. Without peasants, the success of the Greater Poland Uprising would not be possible. Their heroism and devotion to their homeland should be duly commemorated on the anniversary of the celebrations. In the middle of 1918 it was not known whether Wielkopolska would ever be within the borders of the Republic of Poland. The breakthrough was the heroic break of the insurgent army at the end of December, initiated by the famous performance by Ignacy Paderewski in Poznań. After 52 days of fighting, almost the whole Wielkopolska was incorporated into the borders of the Second Republic of Poland. In May 1919, the Greater Poland Army was subordinated to the head of state to Józef Piłsudski, and on 1 June 1919 the election to the Legislative Sejm was distributed in the region. Today, it is hard to imagine what Poland would look like, its latest story without Wielkopolska. You can even risk saying that there would be no Poland on the map today. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u239	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! High Seym! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, I would like to present a government bill amending the law on local taxes and charges contained in print no. 1877. The project aims, starting in 2018, to introduce an obligation for the tax authorities of municipalities to report annually to the Minister responsible for public finances selected information on real estate, agricultural and forestry taxes which are not collected in the framework of the budgetary reporting of local government units. The draft report will contain aggregated information on the tax bases of these taxes and tax bases exempt from these taxes under the resolutions of the municipalities' councils. Information will also be provided on the amount of real estate tax rates, the average price of rye and the average selling price of wood used for agricultural and forestry taxes in the municipality concerned. At the same time, the draft proposes to divide the report into two parts and different deadlines for its transmission, as is apparent from the different dates for the availability of the data covered by the report. Information on tax rates will be provided by the end of the first quarter of the tax year at the latest, while information on tax bases and tax bases will be provided by the end of the third quarter of the tax year at the latest. The information contained in the report shall be communicated only electronically through the Regional Financial Chambers. The detailed scope of the information covered by the report, the model of the report, the deadlines and the manner in which it is transmitted and the manner in which it is drawn up will be determined by the Minister responsible for public finances by means of a regulation. Ladies and gentlemen, I would like to stress that the proposed project is the implementation of the demands of the local government. The joint government and local government committee has given a positive opinion on this project. The data collected on the basis of the proposed report will significantly increase the capacity to analyse current tax arrangements for real estate taxation and municipal tax policy. Dear Marshal, High Izbo! By passing the bill to the High House for consideration, I hope that it will be welcomed and that work on it will be carried out smoothly. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u240	Thank you, Minister. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. You have the floor of Mr Jerzy Małecki, the Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u241	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I have the honour to present a statement concerning the government's draft law amending the Act on Local Taxes and Charges. This scope of the draft law fills a specific gap in the area of budgetary reporting of local government units and meets their demands. At present, municipalities are not required to report on real estate tax, agricultural tax and forestry tax, they only provide basic information on the amount of income from these taxes, the effects of lowering the upper limits of tax rates and the benefits and exemptions granted in these taxes under the resolutions of the municipalities' councils. Other data are obtained by the Ministry of Finance through voluntary surveys carried out every several years. This method involves a considerable amount of work, e.g. with the preparation of forms, the development of a way to collect data, which is a great burden and entails the need to bear high costs. In the opinion of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, the proposed rules respond to this situation. They provide for the obligation to provide the Ministry of Finance only by electronic means, through the RIO, information on the rates of property tax, the average purchase price of rye and the average selling price of wood accepted for tax purposes in the municipality during the tax year. It is useful to assess the fact that, thanks to the data collected in this way, the objective will be achieved in order to increase the capacity to analyse current tax solutions and assess the tax policy of municipalities in terms of using the income potential of real estate taxation. Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! Parliamentary Club Law and Justice welcomes the draft law on amending the law on local taxes and charges and advocates it being directed to continue to process. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u243	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The draft amends the Act on Local Taxes and Charges. They consist in supplementing the provisions relating to property tax, although the amendment concerns not only property tax but also agricultural and forestry tax, maybe not as much as the tax sphere. The draft requires the competent tax authorities to report or report on real estate, agricultural and forestry taxes. This report is to be drawn up, from 2018 onwards, through the Regional Financial Chambers. It will be addressed to the Minister responsible for public finances. According to the draft, the report will contain information on the amount of real estate tax rates, the average rye purchase price, the average selling price for wood - i.e. very relevant data on agricultural tax and forestry tax - as set out in the municipality's resolution and adopted for the purposes of the assessment of those taxes in the municipality, but also information on the tax bases, on tax bases exempt from tax. The reports will be transmitted only in electronic form, as I have already said, to the Ministry of Finance via RIO. The existing IT system is to be used for reporting. The Minister has been authorised to issue a regulation laying down the technical details relating to the drawing up and transmission of the report. The project deserves approval, although it means imposing new obligations on municipalities. Furthermore, Mr PiS said that these surveys were labour-intensive for the Ministry and meant costs, so it is easier if it is done by municipalities and not by the Ministry. But it must be understood that the Ministry of Finance, which is to initiate a policy, even in the field of local taxation law, should have full material in this regard. I believe that it is also important to ensure that this information can be used widely not only by the Ministry but also by local authorities, for which it will also be important information about what is happening in other local government units to such an important extent. I think that this is a request that can be met in the manner of issuing the relevant provisions, but also without such a mode. Like I said, this project deserves approval. We believe that it is important in this respect that local authorities should also be able to use this data. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u273	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present a draft law amending the Corporate Income Tax Act and the Personal Income Tax Act. It is a complex of provisions aimed primarily at sealing the income tax system. Over the last term of office, we have seen a significant drop in income from this in relation to GDP. The aim of the Act is to restore this relationship to adequate levels and in accordance with the European standard. The gasification of the income tax system proposed in this Act is carried out by implementing a number of provisions created specifically for the situation in which our economy is in place, but also by implementing a European Union directive, called the ATAD Directive, which also goes towards sealing the income tax collection system. Our proposal also includes a number of business facilitations, mainly aimed at small and medium-sized enterprises. The Act also amends the tax-free amount, i.e. those who are not engaged in business activities, and changes aimed at enabling taxable persons to submit a number of notices and declarations by electronic means. We are also changing - we can even say that we are simplifying - the clearing of provisions for bad loans in banks and adapting the rules already technically to the new classification of fixed assets. With regard to the main trend of changes, namely the sealing element, the first basic thing that this law introduces is the separation of the calculation of the company's performance account, the VAT taxpayer, into two baskets of revenue and costs. Unfortunately, the operations in the field of capital transactions, whether real or created by the company, often with negative results, were compensated for by operating revenues. The effect of these activities was that the operating income company, by creating losses in the capital account at the end of the day, showed zero or minimal income from its operating activities, thus reducing the tax base in the income tax in an unauthorised manner. In many European countries, it is possible to risk that most of these baskets are distributed, which makes companies pay income tax on the value of their real operating income in a manner that is appropriate to the scale of that income. On the other hand, losses or gains in the equity account relating to shares or other equity instruments compensate and this account is conducted separately, and there, of course, revenue, possibly losses, can be shown and accounted for on a general basis. It can be said that this is an accounting similar to what we observe in ordinary taxpayers, individuals, where if I earn in the form of a salary, on an account, but lost on the stock market, I have no right to compensate for these two economic events and I pay PIT tax on my salary, regardless of the fact that in the tax, so-called. Belka tax, on capital gains I have a loss during this settlement period. Thus, this basic separation aims to eliminate capital costs, which often reduce the positive economic result of the business operations. Another thing is to modify the provisions on interest on loans. Today's legislation, which has been amended so far many times, seems to have failed to meet expectations and, unfortunately, have proved ineffective due to quite simple methods that have allowed these restrictions to be observed. Following the OECD recommendations, we introduce restrictions that seem to be more effective. This is another element, so-called. thin capitalization, but there is also a specific provision for limiting the instrument that allows the transfer of interest costs from the financing of acquisitions of companies or holdings to those operating activities downstairs. It also seems that this possibility unfortunately causes abuse and illegally reduces the tax base carried out by companies in Poland. We also modify the regulations for a controlled foreign company. These rules were introduced a few years ago and it can be said that they did not meet their expectations. The amounts resulting from this were disregarded. It also seems that the expectation of the preventive advantage of these provisions has not been met and has not led to the fact that the domicil of these companies is back in Poland. It seems that the provisions we propose will be more effective in preventing significant assets from being transferred to foreign companies. We are also modifying the tax rules on capital groups. There are several detailed solutions that we propose. On the one hand, we loosen up a little bit the corset of these provisions in order to enable taxpayers to use this instrument more frequently. On the other hand, we eliminate an extraordinary gap that persisted, despite the signals about abuse of the so-called. donations within capital groups, this is about completely fictitious transactions that allowed to avoid taxation in Poland. This gap is also eliminated by these regulations. We also introduce a limit on the cost of obtaining income related to certain intangible services: as we limit interest, so we believe that certain categories of intangible costs should have their limit and be referred to the category of economic profitability of the company. We introduce a minimum income tax for entities with commercial and office real estate above the threshold of PLN 10 million. We believe that the mechanism allowing for crediting this minimum tax on ordinary CIT tax causes us to seal the taxation of profitable properties, which so far, as experience teaches, to a very limited extent bear the burden of taxation in Poland. We have a number of provisions concerning, for example, an increase in the flat rate for real estate rented by individuals. Unfortunately, we have observed a situation in which - a small but yet - percentage of people who benefit from flat-rate taxation of this income abuse this preference by repeatedly renting out many apartments, which already brings this activity closer to full-scale business activity and it seems that this privilege 8,5% the rate of taxation of these revenues is not entitled. We also have a number of rules on taxpayers, individuals, and specifically we are increasing the tax-free amount to 8 000. zł. We also have a doubling of the cost limit for creators. Returning to business: we have an increase in the value or limit of a fixed asset subject to one-off depreciation. This is an important increase, because you can say three times: out of 3.5 thousand. PLN currently up to a round sum of 10,000. zł. Let me remind you that in another law we also allow one-off depreciation of up to 100,000. PLN fixed assets, production, modern robots. Therefore, it can be said that in some space we have very pro-investmental tax treatment of expenditure. We exempt the payment of an advance on income tax when the advance does not exceed PLN 1000. This is such a technical simplification. We have a simplification regarding transfer pricing documentation. We remove the obligation if the only condition for the preparation of transfer pricing documentation is a capital link through the State Treasury, which in principle does not lead to restrictions or erosion of the tax base, which undermines the sense of applying transfer pricing documentation in this respect. We also foresee a revision of the rules on the costing of bank reserves by aligning the reserves prepared for accounting purposes with the tax treatment of those reserves in certain categories. We predict that the sum of these changes next year will bring about PLN 1 billion and a hundred and several tens of millions to the state budget for local authorities. Yes, thank you.

The proposed law is a further step towards the recovery of tax revenues, in particular CIT revenue. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u289	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Polish Free and Solidarni Circle, I present my position on the Government's draft law amending the Personal Income Tax Act, the Corporate Income Tax Act and the Flat-rate Income Tax Act on certain income generated by individuals, print No 1878. In our view, the bill submitted after the entry into force will have a positive impact on the conditions of operation and the development of small and medium-sized enterprises, in particular those carried out in the form of individual activities and personal companies. Essentially, the ability to do so will be limited. aggressive tax optimization, which is currently mainly used by large operators. Thus, large companies will not be able to obtain an unjustified competitive advantage over other market participants. This will bring together the conditions of competitiveness for all companies operating on the Polish market. The bill is quite extensive and contains solutions that can be divided into six groups of rules. The most important issue regulated in the project is the sealing of the corporate income tax system, so as to ensure that the amount of tax paid, especially by large multinational corporations, is linked to the actual place where they earn their income. Studies are known to indicate that the so-called. Luxembourg optimization cost Poland PLN 20 billion per year. The subject of the European Parliament's proceedings in 2014 was the so-called scandal. tax optimisation by more than 340 large global companies, which could cost 27 other Member States as much as €150 billion per year. In order to prevent aggressive tax optimization, the proposed project provides for solutions such as the separation in the Corporate Income Tax Act of the source of income in the form of capital gains and the separation of income from other taxable income or the modification of provisions limiting interest deducted in the form of debt financing costs. In addition, the draft law introduces the required provisions governing the write-down of banks' reserves and write-downs to potential credit losses and to the benefit of small and medium-sized enterprises, provisions aimed at facilitating business activities, including those which simplify and clarify current regulations. The Act will implement the announcements expressed in the exposé by Prime Minister Beata Szydło concerning the increase to 8 thousand. PLN of the tax-free amount will therefore no longer be subject to taxation of an amount below the minimum existence for a single household. It will be increased by 100% , up to PLN 85 528 per year, the limit for applying the rate of 50% the cost of obtaining revenue for creators and favourable provisions will be introduced to extend the titles of exemptions and deductions from income. Thus, the government of Prime Minister Beata Szydło implements the idea of social solidarity. It should be noted that for the proposed positive changes to the bill to prove sustainable, with a tendency to improve for the benefit of citizens and at the same time for the state budget, they need to be introduced gradually. Therefore, the suggestions made by the opposition Members showing that these solutions are not going too far are unjustified. I think it is important to act effectively, gradually reaching a level that can be implemented for the state budget. In addition, the draft law submitted also contains technical provisions enabling PIT taxpayers to submit electronic statements and notifications using designs announced by regulation and to adapt the provisions of the PIT and CIT Act to the new Classification of Durable Measures 2016. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The Free and Solidarni Circle will strongly support this bill. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u319	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! I would like to present a government bill amending the Retail Tax Act, contained in print No. 1879. The Act of 6 July 2016 introduced a new tax on retail sales into the Polish tax system. Its adoption was preceded by a broad public consultation and met with the acceptance of most traders. The purpose of introducing a new tax, in addition to increasing the tax revenue of the State budget, was to compensate for the budgetary damage resulting from the optimisation practices of large retailers in corporate income tax. However, by decision of 19 September 2016, the European Commission initiated a detailed investigation into our tax. The Commission was concerned that progressive revenue-based rates confer a selective advantage on low-income entrepreneurs over their competitors, in breach of EU State aid rules. At the same time, the European Commission issued an order requiring Poland to suspend the application of this tax until the Commission has completed its analysis. In order to avoid the negative financial consequences of the application of the Act in the event of a unfavourable settlement of the dispute with the European Commission, temporary arrangements were then taken. On 18 October 2016, the Minister for Development and Finance issued a regulation on the withdrawal of retail tax, covering the tax due from September to December of that year. The Act subsequently adopted by the Sejm on 15 November 2016 suspended the application of the Retail Tax Act until the end of 2017. We anticipated that the possibility of collecting tax under the rules laid down in the Retail Tax Act would be definitively resolved by that date. We were then in the process of making a complaint to the Court of Justice of the European Union for the Commission's decision on the Polish retail tax, which was finally submitted by Poland on 30 November 2016. It therefore appeared that 31 December 2017 was a realistic deadline for the conclusion of the dispute as to the possibility of collecting the tax in question. On 30 June 2017, the European Commission issued a final decision on the Polish retail tax and decided, contrary to our expectations, that this tax infringes EU State aid rules. The Commission concluded that, thanks to progressive tax rates based on the size of the low turnover company's revenues, it had an advantage over its competitors. In the Commission's view, Poland did not demonstrate that the progressive tax rates were justified by the retail tax objective in terms of revenue increases or that companies at a higher rate would be more able to pay the tax. Poland disagrees with the European Commission's assessment of the Polish retail tax in the context of State aid rules. On 13 September 2017, therefore, an action was also brought before the Court of Justice of the European Union for a decision of the European Commission of 30 June 2017. There is a risk that Poland's complaints will not be recognised until the end of the period for suspending the application of the Retail Tax Act, i.e. until the end of 2017. It is therefore necessary to extend the suspension of its application for the following year. Without a ruling by the European Union court, the collection of retail tax under the rules laid down in the Retail Tax Act could put the State Treasury at risk of loss. The solutions proposed in the project will continue to suspend the application of the Retail Tax Act for 2018. As a result, this tax will only be levied on retail sales revenue achieved from 1 January 2019. We expect the court to decide within this time limit, which will allow for the collection of the suspended tax. Dear Marshal! High Izbo! By passing the bill to the High House for consideration, I hope that it will be accepted with understanding and that work on it will proceed smoothly. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
