ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u336	I present the position of the Poselski Kukiz Club15 vis-à-vis the government's draft law amending the Retail Tax Act, which concerns the extension of the suspension of the retail tax Act until the end of 2018. The European Commission initiated a detailed investigation into the Polish retail tax, i.e. the impact on competition between sales entities on the Polish market. Poland has undertaken to suspend the application of this tax until the Commission has completed its analysis. The Retail Tax Act raised doubts, in particular as regards the formula for the application of turnover tax on sales in the context of conditions of equal opportunities for market competition. Club Kukiz'15 made a lot of comments on the bill. Retail sale under the law is the exercise in the territory of the Republic of Poland, within the context of the business of the seller, of the sale of goods to consumers, and the retailer is a natural person, a legal person, a civil company and an organizational unit which does not have a legal personality selling the goods to the consumer. Purchases from an intermediary in the sale of goods, e.g. An agent or a police officer is also to be taxed. The retail tax represents the cost of obtaining income in personal income tax and corporate income tax. The retail tax obligation applies to retailers who achieve revenue in a given month exceeding PLN 17 million. Income above this amount is to be taxed. The amount of the tax is to depend on the entity's monthly turnover: -0.8% for revenue up to PLN 170 million, - 1.4% for surplus income over PLN 170 million. The Ministry of Finance itself pointed out: With such a structure defining a taxpayer, a company with a high income in order to bypass the retail tax or lower its rate may attempt to divide into several smaller entities. And we're already dealing with that fact. Further, according to the opinion of the Ministry: The entry into force of the new public service tax, which is the retail tax, may result in a search for the possibility of optimization by taxpayers, which may be manifested inter alia. development of online trade as not regulated by law or legal separation of retail entities. The tax on retail sales will not be included in the revenue generated from sales made on the basis of a distance contract, i.e. via the Internet. The solutions finally adopted in the Act do not provide for taxation of franchise networks. The fact is that the introduction of the so-called. turnover tax for online commerce will affect the formal side of sales. Foreign companies - other than those registered in Poland - will offer their products from outside Poland without transferring their warehouse and logistics facilities. In conclusion, it is expected that when the law is suspended, this situation will affect the transfer of place of business abroad. The Poselski Kukiz Club15 is in favour of extending the suspension of the retail tax law until real concerns about the possible reduction of revenue from the tax on goods and services and income taxes as a result of the application of that law are resolved. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u339	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present a bill dedicated to tax fraud, in particular VAT fraud. The project counters this pathology, builds on the side of the tax administration the potential, tools and resources of data that will prevent the flow of money from these crimes abroad. Despite the follow-up of the checks and the identification of the tax offence, this exit is irretrievably depriving us of our chances of recovering lost public finances. The Act creates a system for the exchange of information between the banking sector and the tax administration, which allows the tax administration to carry out analyses of financial flows so that, through appropriate mechanisms, the analysis of the financial flows themselves, as well as data from other sources and databases of the Ministry of Finance, identify such flows which are symptomatic for tax fraud, identify them and react in advance. STIR, i.e. the IT system of the clearing house, is intended to enable the National Tax Administration to provide relevant data for timely responses. On the other hand, the Act allows the banking system itself to carry out analyses that aim to clear the system of bank accounts from bank accounts established by the so-called "bank accounts." poles, i.e. fictitiously created entities, only for the purpose of carrying out a tax offence. Therefore, already in the first stage of preparation for fiscal crime, these preparations will be hindered. The Act also provides for the publication and full transparency of entities deleted from the list of active VAT taxable persons, so that each trader is able to verify whether his counterparty is currently not deleted from the VAT list and, on the other hand, if the trader has previously been deleted from the VAT list, it will be a symptom to increase his diligence or to increase his caution on the part of the trader in carrying out transactions with this type of entity. The STIR system or just this act creating the STIR system is a key element of sealing the tax system outside the invoice register, which will enter from 1 January next year outside the split payment mechanism, which is to enter from 1 April. The STIR system complements these sealing instruments because no single system or single seal design is fully functional and efficient and the state must seek parallel complementary instruments to eliminate VAT fraud pathology. This is an outline of the STIR project. Its basic functional layer is the technical provisions on how to prepare, collect, transmit and process information at two levels, both in the clearing house and on the state side in state systems. Another group of rules concerns the possibility of blocking transactions or financial flows that have potential here and where there is a risk of non-returning money flow abroad. Of course, the rules on blocking these flows are mitigated with appropriate appeal possibilities. The third group of regulations, of course, creates the appropriate supervision of the head of the KAS over the entire information exchange process. And, of course, we have a group of rules that allow the finance minister to publish a list of deleted entities. So that this is from the perspective of the Ministry of Finance one of the key laws leading to the sealing of the system and the execution of the assumed budget revenue for this purpose next year. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u340	Thank you very much. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. Ms Iwona Michalek, Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, will be the first to speak.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u341	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I have the honour on behalf of the Law and Justice Club to present a position on the Act amending the laws in order to prevent the use of the financial sector for tax fraud, print No 1880. The main objective of this draft law is to reduce the VAT gap caused by fraud. The project introduces a system that eliminates the economic turnover of businesses that deceive honest entrepreneurs and thus strengthens the security of VAT taxpayers in the economic trade. The project will translate into improved business conditions for all taxpayers by restoring fair competition on the market. The simplification to the VAT gap consists of the value of fraud and the shortage caused by non-notified sales, so-called "tax." It's a gray area. The value of fraud represents a real loss of the VAT budget in the form of amounts reimbursed for artificial transactions called carousel transactions. The only purpose of these transactions is to request unjustified VAT refunds. Carousel transactions are organised by criminal groups and involve committing a number of fiscal and criminal offences. There are different methodologies for calculating the value of VAT fraud. It is certainly possible to determine its lower value. It currently amounts to not less than PLN 10 billion per year. According to the figures of the tax services for the years 2015 and 2016, the amount of VAT fraud identified by these services amounted to almost PLN 20 billion, but this amount only reflects the scale of identified and documented fraud. Therefore, it can be assumed that the actual scale of fraud is much larger. The VAT gap is largely due to organised criminal groups, which deliberately exploit legislative shortcomings or the weakness of law enforcement by law enforcement and justice. The effects of the tax gap, among others, The loss of revenue of the State Treasury, which is a consequence of tax fraud in relation to VAT, is borne by all citizens of the Republic of Poland. There is no doubt about the need to urgently and decisively counter the use of the financial sector for tax fraud. The expected impact of the project is to increase the activity of banks and cooperative savings and credit banks in the area of risk analysis of the use of their systems for illegal activities, in particular VAT fraud, the sealing of the tax system, the reduction of the grey and black zone, the deletion from the VAT registers of entities whose main purpose is to fake business activities in order to extort VAT returns, to identify others outside tax areas at risk of committing financial crimes, to increase cooperation with other countries in the fight against cross-border crime, including organised crime, and, of course, to prevent money laundering. This is a very necessary law, one of the most important, in my opinion, legislation, which seals the banking, tax system. Therefore, the Law and Justice Club welcomes the draft submitted and proposes that it be further examined. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u346	Thank you very much, Member. Please, Mrs Genowefa Tokarska, the Polish People's Party.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u347	Thank you. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the pleasure on behalf of the Polish People's Party Parliamentary Club to express my position on the project from print no. 1880. The proposed project is quite extensive due to its scope of impact, brings changes and regulations to a very large number of provisions of the applicable law. The need for these changes is due to the existence of tax fraud, particularly in the tax on goods and services, using banks and SKOKs, the possibility of a rapid flow of funds and the possibility of them being carried out, i.e. tax fraud. The matter of this bill is quite difficult and complicated also due to the protection of personal data, banking and fiscal secrecy, as well as the need to comply with the constitution. You have already expressed doubts about this. However, VAT fraud is a fact and its size results from numerous reports analysing the situation in this area. Depending on the source, the value of VAT fraud in Poland is estimated at PLN 10 and this figure corresponds to the average in the European Union. Currently, the importance of electronic transactions is increasing. The area of these transactions is also in the interest of crime, especially organised crime. Electronic transactions also pose risks to the public sector by using banking systems even to legitimise activities that are actually intended only to extort tax. The proposal proposes that the identification of risks be based more than previously on an analysis of the risk of using bank accounts and SKOKs for purposes related to tax offences, in particular VAT, and other related offences, e.g. by issuing empty invoices. The project involves an analysis by the Head of National Tax Administration of the risk of using banks or SKOKs to commit tax offences. The risk analysis will be carried out taking into account the risk ratio established by the clearing house in its electronic system, called STIR. In the current state of technical, information and social development, the analysis of financial flows is one of the most important methods for identifying risks. In VAT carousels for VAT refunds, transfers of funds through subsequent accounts are most often carried out using the banking system. Thus, the project provides for the head of the National Tax Administration to carry out analyses of the risk of using banks for extortion purposes, to automatically establish in STIR a risk indicator for the use of banks, to require banks and cooperative credit and savings banks to report data to STIR, including those that constitute bank secrecy or professional secrecy respectively, in order to establish a risk indicator, to provide information by the clearing house on the risk indicator to the head of the National Tax Administration, as well as to banks and SKOKs for the purpose of carrying out certain obligations, inter alia. resulting from the Anti-Money Laundering and Terrorist Financing Act, electronically keeping lists of entities which have not been registered as VAT payers or have been removed from the register ex officio without having to be notified of this or have been reinstated as VAT taxpayers. The so-called VAT fraud is often used in criminal cases. A vanishing taxpayer. In such situations, it is crucial to take immediate action to secure the means to be used to pay the tax due. It's a project, as I emphasized, very extensive. Other countries at European Union level also address similar issues. [[♪ The bell ♪]] However, the costs of these solutions are quite high, but this may already be in a series of questions. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u348	Thank you very much, Member. Please, Mr Ireneusz Gains, the Free and Solidarnia Members' Circle.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u349	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Freedom and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the government draft law amending laws to prevent the use of the financial sector for tax fraud, print No 1880. The most important objective pursued by this draft law is to reduce the VAT gap caused by fraud. The project will lead to a significant sealing of the VAT system through an analysis of the financial flows that occur in the setting up of bank accounts by various entities, including those aimed at extortion of VAT, without adversely affecting the operation of a fair business. There is no doubt about the need to urgently and decisively counter the use of the broadly understood financial sector for tax fraud, in particular VAT and other related offences, e.g. consisting in displaying the so-called "exhibition." empty invoices. The fight against this problem has been going on for many years, but for various reasons its effectiveness is not sufficient, as confirmed by, among other things. NIK report on empty invoices. In 2016, VAT revenues amounted to PLN 127 billion, this year they can reach up to PLN 150 billion. In 2015 and 2016, the tax control stated a reduction in budget revenue by about PLN 19.8 billion as a result of VAT fraud. One of the popular mechanisms for VAT fraud is so-called. tax carousels. They rely on the fact that the goods in a series of transactions are circulating many times between often fictional companies, and each time a disappearing taxable person is involved in the transaction, who charges VAT but does not pay it to the tax office. In such situations, it is important to take rapid action to secure the means to be used to pay the tax due. In order to end this criminal activity, two solutions have been proposed in the draft law: the mechanism for blocking the account at the request of the head of the National Tax Administration - measures secured in this way will be released if the tax due resulting from the invoices issued is paid - and the refusal to register and the removal from office of the entity as a VAT taxable person without the need to notify that entity. As technology develops, the role of banks is increasing as the number and importance of non-cash transactions, including by electronic means, is increasing. Currently, financial systems allow for rapid financial transfers of significant amounts, which can be used to create appearances of doing business. These circumstances are used by organised criminal groups to commit tax offences and then to legalize the money unduly obtained, so-called. money laundering. This increases the role and importance of risk analyses on the basis of data obtained from the banking sector, including cooperative savings and credit banks. The proposal proposes that the identification of risks in both sectors should be based more than previously on an analysis of the risk of using banking systems for purposes related to fiscal offences against tax obligations. The risk analysis will be carried out by the head of the National Tax Administration, which will use two tools for this purpose: own analysis carried out by the office of the head of the KAS and its subordinate entities based on data from banks and SKOKs and data from tax returns and other publicly available sources and analysis prepared by the clearing house, which will establish in its electronic system called STIR a risk indicator based on data from banks and SKOKs and other publicly available data. In addition, the project provides for a number of other mechanisms, including: providing information by the clearing house on the risk ratio of the head of the KAS, providing information by the Board on the risk ratio of banks and SKOKs in order to comply with the obligations under the Anti-Money Laundering and Terrorist Financing Act, etc. It should be considered that the proposed law provides the basis for a more effective defence of the fiscal interests of the State, so that the State Treasury's tax claims will be better protected against fraud, which in the last 10 years have been at a level of up to PLN 40 billion per year. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The Free and Solidarni Circle will strongly support this project [[♪ The bell ♪]] Laws. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

So they often have much more efficient teams, more computerized, faster internationally than the tax administration, which, for example, encounters difficulties in communicating with other tax administrations, because this EU information exchange system is not optimal or far from optimal or adequate to the rate of economic turnover used by criminal groups. So this project is hitting this asymmetry by building a symmetrical tool for both data processing and blocking instruments in a situation where we are sure that there is extortion. I am therefore convinced that this is an appropriate tool, proportionate to the challenges we currently have. As regards the consultation layer, the project was consulted, we have many comments from entrepreneurs and business organisations, and NGOs, which deal with information from the citizen. Only the result of the analysis is distributed further within the tax administration, there is no general access of a serial official to the transfer data. I'd like to rule that out. We are therefore not afraid of this charge. With regard to the question of whether the interference of blocking funds on the bank account is an interference with the right of ownership of the entrepreneur and the citizen, the answer is: of course, yes. This is the most drastic of possible interferences that can be imagined, apart from the execution from the bank account, and we believe that this instrument is proportionate to the intended effects of this law. As far as jobs are concerned, we have 95 analytical posts planned for the tax administration. As far as the courts are concerned, a dozen or so jobs are planned. It seems to me that these analytical posts are something that is absolutely necessary for these services to be professionalized for a long time. As far as costs for the financial sector are concerned, it must be said here that clearing the banking system from these poles accounts and clearing the banking system from this pathology is to the great benefit of the banking sector not counting in millions of zlotys of implementation costs, only in much larger amounts, thanks to which banks can save on lending activities that seemingly seem fair and ultimately turns out to be a crime, loans disappear. It has often been the case that the carousels were also financed by the financial sector, so it can be said that both in the economy and in the banking sector itself, the security of trade will increase. And not just because of our decisions, it's just the way it is. Therefore, the operation of this smaller banking activity on separate IT systems is simply ineffective and can be improved, and we are also in dialogue with the sector in order to improve this. How will this mechanism help honest taxpayers? The main element of this system is that there will be a faster elimination of those involved, which is intended to increase the safety of trading throughout the economy. And an honest trader will simply, after sealing the VAT system, have a much easier and safer contract with tenderers he does not know and we will facilitate business activity in this sense. [[But the Member left, so I don't think he wanted to hear it.]] As far as the locking mechanism is concerned, it is quite complex and based on risk analysis. We estimate that this type of blockage will be several hundred throughout the year. I assure you that each of these blockades will be based on an analysis of both the STIR data, the central invoice register and other databases of the Ministry of Finance. It will be done in the preparatory phase automatically. Any decision to block shall be taken under the responsibility of the relevant official after manual checking. This system is designed to improve the efficiency and efficiency of the blockades, so that these locks, where today there is a smaller source resource causing the lock decision, are better hit. In fact, we believe that this system will lead to more efficiency and fewer errors, which, of course, have to occur statistically with today's mass of cases. Of course, we write laws on appeal because we do not assume full, 100% correctness of the decision. Unfortunately, this is never the case, of course. After 72 hours on the basis of other conditions, more qualified, we can extend this blockade for a further period. At each stage we guarantee an appeal with a very short time of reaction of the courts counted in days, which will secure, we hope, the interest of the taxpayer. Nevertheless, I believe that the integration of the Central Register of Invoices system, STIR, will result in a high precision of these blockades. Is the system needed? I agree that this system would be unnecessary. In this situation where we are, this system is needed. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u367	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! Sandomierskie Collegium Gostomianum is one of the oldest and most prestigious Polish high schools. Its beginning dates back to 1602, hence on the last Saturday, 6 October 2017, the celebrations of the 415th anniversary of the school connected with the 10th anniversary of its graduates took place. It was attended by nearly 500 people from all over Poland, but also from many other countries around the world. The Collegium Gostomianum was founded as a Jesuit school, and its name is due to the founder, who was the Poznań Voivode and at the same time the castle of the Sandomierz Hieronim Gostomski coat of arms Nałęcz. It was thanks to him that a large monastery team was erected in the years 1602-1615, consisting of two wings: the eastern one, intended for the school, and the southern one, containing the monastery clausz. After several years, the monastery became a very dynamic educational and economic center. The Jesuit college here had a rich library, a school theater functioned, and the monastery vineyards functioned splendidly. However, the development of the school stopped the Swedish flood, during which both the college and Sandomierz were badly destroyed. It took years to rebuild the school because of lack of resources. Despite this, the school printing house was opened in 1712 and by 1774, it had issued as many as 167 items. After the Order's cassation in 1773, the monastery went to the Board of the National Education Commission. The preserved east wing was constantly occupied by the school, while the south, destroyed by fire in 1813, despite several attempts to adapt, fell into ruin. During World War II, the building of the Collegium Gostomianum was occupied for military purposes, and after the war until 1986 there was the First General High School. However, the poor technical condition of the building caused that the study was held in another place at ul. The carpenters. In September 2001, the high school returned to its headquarters after several years of renovation. A year later, a gym was put into service, located in a partially reconstructed south wing. Today, the building of the Collegium Gostomianum is one of Sandomierz's most valuable monuments, and it is a great dominant feature in the panorama of the city from the Vistula side. The Sandomierz Collegium Gostomianum boasts many outstanding graduates. You can't name them all, so I'm just gonna limit myself to a few names. They graduated from the school, among others. ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u371	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! There are events in personal, professional, social or political life that not only leave a lasting mark in our memory, but - which, in my view, is a much more serious issue - decisively determine the future of people. Unfortunately, Poles cannot complain about the lack of bad experiences. In our Polish history there are many dates that could decide about the existence of the Polish state, so it is our duty to constantly remind them. Today I would like to recall the date of 17 September 1939, on which 78 years ago - despite the important pact on non-aggression between Poland and the Union of Soviet Socialist Republics of 1932, which was applicable until 1945 - the Soviet troops entered the eastern territory of the Second Republic. The decision to withdraw from the findings contained in the non-aggression pact and to enter the territory of the Republic of Poland was the consequence of the so-called non-aggression pact signed on 23 August 1939. The Ribbentrop-Molotov Pact, by which the fourth partition of Poland and the division of Central Europe into the zones of influence of two countries began de facto. With a great deal of knowledge today about the behaviour of European politicians before World War II, we can safely say that Soviet aggression against Poland was planned much earlier in 1939. This is evidenced by a meeting in Moscow on 4 October 1938 - i.e. a few days after the signing of the Munich Treaty - the Deputy Minister for Foreign Affairs of the Soviet Union Vladimir Potimkin with the French ambassador, at which the Russian government said: I see no other way out than for us the fourth partition of Poland. After such words and disregardful treatment of the behaviour of the politicians of Germany and the Union of Soviet Socialist Republics by states that mattered in the political market of the world, it had already to be assumed that no political agreement would be honoured by the aggressors. Poland was not only to disappear from the maps of the world, but also to become a Polish socialist republic between the Vistula and Bug. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The entry of the Red Army into the territory of the Second Republic of Poland on 17 September 1939 and the violation of the Polish-Soviet non-aggression pact was surprising enough for the Polish government that he did not, however, decide to declare war to the aggressor. Nor did such a decision be decided by an hour's notice. 3 on the night of September 17, 1939, by the Deputy National Commissioner - Minister - Foreign Affairs of the Union of Soviet Socialist Republics Potemkin to the Ambassador of the Republic of Poland in Moscow Wacław Grzybowski diplomatic note, in which the Russian party unilaterally stated the cessation of the existence of the Polish state. The consequence of such an absurd assumption - as it was written, justifying its behaviour, in a note - was entering Polish eastern lands to protect the property and life of Ukrainians and Belarusians living there. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The September campaign ended in a defeat of Poland. The consequences of this disaster are known. Therefore, let us constantly remind about its effects, because we see how similarly the Russian authorities justify each of their present armed intervention against nations wishing to develop their independent national culture. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-10-11-sejm-49-2.u373	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! In my statement, I want to share the impressions I took from the ceremony of swearing in the Territorial Defence Army, which took place on 23 September this year. In Nisko. Over 140 soldiers, 32. The Battalion of the Light Infantry, which is already stationed in the Transnian garrison, was the day it vowed. This celebration impressed me very much. She was incredibly worthy. I think that's the word to be used here. The four verses of the hymn were sung with all strength not only by volunteers, not only by soldiers of the Territorial Defence Army, but also by guests and residents of Niska, parents of these soldiers. It is really rare, because, ladies and gentlemen, I am very pleased that it is in such a dignified way that these young people enter into such a serious service, a service for the Republic of Poland. This event merely confirmed my conviction that the Territorial Defence Army was needed in Poland, but convinced those who had so far been skeptical about the creation of this new type of Armed Forces. I later spoke to the people of Niska, who now appreciate this formation very well. Moreover, from national research it appears that the Territorial Defence Forces are well-connected and Poles asked whether they should be created, confirm, say so, that in every voivodship the brigade should be created. And that is the intention of the Ministry of National Defence. From this point on, I want to thank all those who form the Territorial Defence Army. I thank Minister Antoni Macierewicz for his idea and for his consistent implementation. Thank you, General Kukule, who is the boss and who is in charge of this formation. The general has served in the Special Forces before, so that I am convinced that his experience and skills in this field will be able to pass on to these young people, so that they will be really very professionally trained. The territorial defence forces are intended to serve the local community in times of peace, help in the elimination of natural disasters, but also cooperate with local government in various situations, and in the event of war - and I hope that this will not happen in Poland - of course they are to defend the local population. By 2019 there will be 16 brigades in every voivodship. That's over 53,000. soldiers. It's a huge force, mostly young people, who will build our defense potential. Once again, I encourage all those who love the Republic who feel up to serving our country to engage in the Territorial Defence Army. I know that in WCU Low recruitment is still ongoing, so that I encourage all those who would like to try their strength here. As I say, I am convinced that these troops will fulfil their function and will be a very strong reinforcement of our professional army. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
